[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '28,100p' .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review-presentation.txt
The dispatched prompt body may contain one structured marker block. Parse it before applying defaults.

```yaml
PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS:
  review_target: <spec-folder>
  review_target_type: spec-folder  # one of: spec-folder | skill | agent | track | files
  review_dimensions: all  # or comma-separated list: any of correctness, security, traceability, maintainability, plus custom names that follow them
  review_focus: ""  # optional free text every iteration covers in addition to its dimension
  spec_folder: existing  # one of: existing | new | update-related | phase-folder, or an explicit specs/.opencode/specs path
  execution_mode: AUTONOMOUS  # from :auto suffix
 lineage_mode: auto  # one of: auto | resume | restart | completed-continue (set by --extend=N)
 extend: ""  # optional positive integer N from --extend=N: extends a completed run by N iterations
 extend_minutes: ""  # optional positive integer M from --extend-minutes=M: minutes added to the duration budget, default the prior budget
  maxIterations: 7
  convergenceThreshold: 0.10
  convergence_mode: default  # default | off | sliding-window | divergent
  depth_mode: default  # default | expansive (opt-in frontier growth)
  frontier_scope: none  # internal, written by fan-out for an expansive lineage: comma-separated frontier node ids, or none
  stop_policy: convergence  # one of: convergence | max-iterations
  executor: native  # one of: native | cli-codex | cli-claude-code | cli-opencode | cli-cursor | cli-devin | cli-pi | cli-deepseek-harness | cli-mistral-vibe, the last one fan-out only (`EXECUTOR_KINDS` in `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/executor-config.ts`)
  executor_model: ""  # optional, executor-specific (cli-opencode e.g. llmgateway/glm-5.3-flash, minimax-coding-plan/MiniMax-M2.7-highspeed)
  executor_config_dir: ""  # optional, cli-claude-code only; maps to CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR
  executor_reasoning: ""  # optional
  executor_service_tier: ""  # optional
  executor_timeout: 900  # optional
  resource_map_emit: true  # optional
```

Rules:

- Any unspecified field falls back to its documented default.
- Marker fields take precedence over `$ARGUMENTS` flags because the caller explicitly bound setup in the prompt body.
- Unknown fields are warnings, not errors.
- Malformed lines, including missing `:`, emit a parse error naming the offending line. Known fields parsed before the error may still be used, and unresolved fields continue to Tier 2 or Tier 3.
- Empty strings count as unresolved for required fields.
- Compact legacy answer strings are only for the consolidated `:confirm` prompt. They are not a `:auto` marker format.

### Default Resolution Table

| Field | Required | Resolves Via | Default | Tier-2 Candidate |
|-------|----------|--------------|---------|------------------|
| `review_target` | Y | `$ARGUMENTS` first positional target, or marker `review_target` | none | N |
| `review_target_type` | Y | marker `review_target_type`, or auto-detect from `review_target` | inferred only | Y, when target is present but ambiguous |
| `review_dimensions` | Y | flag `--dims` if supported by caller, marker `review_dimensions`, or default | `"all"` | N |
| `review_focus` | N | flag `--review-focus="TEXT"`, marker `review_focus`, or default. Control characters and whitespace runs collapse to one space, an empty result is absent, and more than 1000 characters fails setup | empty (dimensions only) | N |
| `spec_folder` | Y | flag `--spec-folder`, marker `spec_folder`, `scope-extract` → a target/scope path that resolves to an existing spec folder (auto-bound, per `auto_mode_contract` §1 source 3), or target path when target is a spec folder | none | Y, when target is not a spec folder |
| `execution_mode` | Y | attached suffix `:auto` or marker `execution_mode` | `AUTONOMOUS` under `:auto` | N |
| `lineage_mode` | Y | flag `--restart`, flag `--lineage-mode=auto|resume|restart`, marker `lineage_mode`, or default | `auto` | N |
| `extend` | N | flag `--extend=N` (positive integer); sets `lineage_mode=completed-continue` on a completed run; refused with fan-out | none | N |
| `extend_minutes` | N | flag `--extend-minutes=M` (positive integer), valid only with `--extend` | the prior `maxDurationMinutes` | N |
| `maxIterations` | Y | flag `--max-iterations`, marker `maxIterations`, or default | `7` | N |
| `convergenceThreshold` | Y | flag `--convergence`, marker `convergenceThreshold`, or default | `0.10` | N |
| `convergence_mode` | Y | flag `--convergence-mode=default|off|sliding-window|divergent`, marker `convergence_mode`, or default | `default` | N |
| `depth_mode` | Y | flag `--depth-mode=default|expansive`, marker `depth_mode`, or default `default` | `default` | N |
| `stop_policy` | Y | flag `--stop-policy=convergence|max-iterations`, marker `stop_policy`, or default | `convergence` | N |
| `executor` | N | flag `--executor`, marker `executor`, config file, or default | `native` | N |
| `executor_model` | N | flag `--model`, marker `executor_model`, or executor-specific validation | none | N |
| `executor_config_dir` | N | flag `--config-dir`, marker `executor_config_dir`, or executor-specific default | none | N |
| `executor_reasoning` | N | flag `--reasoning-effort`, marker `executor_reasoning`, or executor default | none | N |
| `executor_service_tier` | N | flag `--service-tier`, marker `executor_service_tier`, or executor default | none | N |
| `executor_timeout` | N | flag `--executor-timeout`, marker `executor_timeout`, or default | `900` | N |
| `resource_map_emit` | N | flag `--no-resource-map`, marker `resource_map_emit`, or default | `true` | N |
| `frontier_scope` | N | internal marker `frontier_scope`, written by `fanout-run.cjs` for an expansive fan-out lineage | none | N |
| `config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir` | N | internal flag `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir=PATH` or marker `config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir` | none | N |
| `fanout_executors` | N | repeatable `--executor=<type>` flags or `--executors=<json>`; each group accepts `--model`, `--config-dir`, `--reasoning-effort`, `--service-tier`, `--executor-timeout`, `--iters`, `--label`, `--count`; one expanded lineage selects `config.executor`, two or more select `config.fanout` | none (single-executor when absent) | N |
| `fanout_concurrency` | N | flag `--concurrency=N` | `2` | N |

**Fan-out default policy:** 0–1 `--executor` flags and no `--executors`, or an `--executors` matrix that expands to exactly one lineage → `config.executor` (single-executor, default, unchanged). 2+ `--executor` flags, an `--executors` matrix that expands to two or more lineages, or any `--count > 1` → `config.fanout`. Review fan-out uses strongest-restriction: any lineage active P0 → merged FAIL. Native and CLI fan-out lineages run under `fanout-run.cjs` in the capped pool so every lineage shares retries, artifact checks, and stop-policy validation. The native adapter calls the `deep/review` command with the internal `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir` override; it must not dispatch the LEAF `@deep-review` agent or a pasted phase-running prompt as a full-loop sub-agent. When fan-out includes `cli-opencode`, that lineage is an explicit parallel-detached OpenCode session with its own lineage directory and session id; this is the documented exception to the cli-opencode self-invocation guard. Single-executor `cli-opencode` remains cross-runtime only.

**Lifecycle policy:** `lineage_mode=auto` preserves the existing classifier behavior, `lineage_mode=resume` continues a valid packet, and `lineage_mode=restart` archives the resolved `{artifact_dir}` before any config write, phase init, or fan-out spawn. In `:auto`, an explicit `--restart` or `--lineage-mode=restart` flag is operator authorization for this archive move, so do not ask for a second confirmation. Preserve rollback by moving the timestamped archive back to `review/` if needed. A restart request must never silently downgrade to resume; if the archive step cannot run, halt with `STATUS=FAIL`.

**Stop policy:** `stop_policy=convergence` preserves normal deep-review behavior: legal convergence may stop before `maxIterations`. `stop_policy=max-iterations` makes convergence signals telemetry-only until the iteration ceiling; before `maxIterations`, broaden the next angle instead of synthesizing. Terminal manual pause, user stop, unrecoverable error, and `maxIterationsReached` still stop.
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '28,100p' .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research-presentation.txt
`--dry-run` binds `dry_run=true` in both execution modes.
Setup then binds its values in memory and skips the Tier 1 config persist, so no `deep-research-config.json` is written.
The workflow then prints `dry_run_halt` preview lines and stops before any dispatch or state write.
Show the operator the halt line and say that nothing was written.
Both execution modes run the telemetry heartbeat and the run-now check.

### PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS Schema (for `:auto` non-interactive dispatch)

The dispatched prompt body may contain one structured marker block. Parse it before applying defaults. Grammar: see `auto-mode-contract.md` §2.

```yaml
PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS:
  research_topic: WebSocket reconnection strategies  # string
  spec_folder: <spec-folder>  # existing | new | update-related | phase-folder | explicit path
  execution_mode: AUTONOMOUS  # from :auto suffix
  maxIterations: 10  # positive integer
  convergenceThreshold: 0.05  # decimal 0..1
  convergence_mode: default  # default | off | sliding-window | divergent
  depth_mode: default  # default | expansive (opt-in frontier growth)
  lineage_mode: auto  # one of: auto | resume | restart | completed-continue (set by --extend=N)
  extend: ""  # optional positive integer N from --extend=N: extends a completed run by N iterations
  extend_minutes: ""  # optional positive integer M from --extend-minutes=M: minutes added to the duration budget, default the prior budget
  executor: native  # native | cli-codex | cli-claude-code | cli-opencode | cli-cursor | cli-devin | cli-pi | cli-deepseek-harness | cli-mistral-vibe, the last one fan-out only (`EXECUTOR_KINDS` in `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/executor-config.ts`)
  executor_model: ""  # optional executor-specific model id (cli-opencode e.g. llmgateway/glm-5.3-flash, minimax-coding-plan/MiniMax-M2.7-highspeed)
  executor_config_dir: ""  # optional, cli-claude-code only; maps to CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR
  executor_reasoning: ""  # optional reasoning effort
  executor_service_tier: ""  # optional service tier
  executor_timeout: 900  # optional positive integer seconds
  resource_map_emit: true  # boolean
```

Rules: see `auto-mode-contract.md` §2 (unspecified fields fall back to default; marker fields take precedence over `$ARGUMENTS` flags; unknown fields warn; malformed lines parse-error).

```
The YAML contains the full loop workflow: initialization, iteration dispatch, convergence detection, synthesis, and memory save.

### Dry-Run Halt

`--dry-run` binds `dry_run=true` in both execution modes.
Setup then binds its values in memory and skips the Tier 1 config persist, so no `deep-review-config.json` is written.
The workflow then prints `dry_run_halt` preview lines and stops before any dispatch or state write.
Show the operator the halt line and say that nothing was written.

### Review Success

```
Deep review complete.
Iterations: [N] | Stop reason: [converged|max_iterations|all_dimensions_clean]
Findings: P0=[N] P1=[N] P2=[N] | Verdict: [PASS|CONDITIONAL|FAIL] [PASS may include hasAdvisories=true]
Artifacts: review/review-report.md, review/resource-map.md (unless `--no-resource-map`), [N] iteration files in review/, continuity update in canonical spec docs refreshed
Ready for: /speckit:plan [remediation] (if FAIL/CONDITIONAL)
STATUS=OK PATH=[spec-folder-path]
```

### What the completion message must carry

A path and a count are not a report. The reader asked a question and the run answered it, so
the answer belongs in the message and the artifact path belongs after it, for going deeper.
Handing over only a path moves the work of reading the report onto the person who asked for it.
=== research 330-360
```

### Memory Integration After Completing

- `node .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/dist/continuity/generate-context.js /tmp/save-context-data-<session-id>.json [spec-folder]`
- Verify that the canonical save routed continuity into the expected packet doc (decision-record.md / implementation-summary.md / handover.md)

### Anchor Tags (Automatic)

`ANCHOR:deep-research-[topic]`, `ANCHOR:findings`, `ANCHOR:convergence-report`

### Error Handling

| Error | Action |
|-------|--------|
| Agent dispatch timeout | Retry once with reduced scope, then mark timeout |
| State file missing | Reconstruct from iteration files |
| 3 consecutive failed iterations | Auto mode: stop with reason `error` and enter synthesis with partial findings. Confirm mode only: a checkpoint first asks Retry, Skip or Cancel |
| Memory save failure | Save to `scratch/` as backup |

#!/usr/bin/env node

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MODULE: Deep-Loop Convergence Entrypoint
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// The stop decision is computed from loop state rather than from prose, so two
// runs over the same state stop at the same point. Its object owns nine fields
// in this order: decision, stopReason, reasonDetail, weightedStopScore, signals,
// gates, blockedBy, graphDecision and inputs. stopReason keeps the frozen
// eight-value set; the finer cause travels in reasonDetail as
// maxIterationsReached, consecutiveFailures, stuckThresholdReached,
// convergenceModeOff, stopPolicyMaxIterations, noStopCandidate,
// minIterationsNotReached, frontierOpen,
// qualityGuardFailed, all_questions_answered, all_dimensions_clean,
// composite_converged, frontier_drained, graphStopBlocked, graph_convergence_continue,
// graphDecisionMissing, or the name of a failed gate.

} = require('../lib/deep-loop/observability-events.cjs');

  'configJson',

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '225,250p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md
Executor settings are owned by the YAML workflow and rendered prompt pack -- never hand-dispatch review iterations; each iteration stays LEAF-only and produces the required markdown plus JSONL delta. Full contract (per-iteration invariants, failure modes, JSONL audit field, config surface/precedence, TrustState surface): `references/protocol/loop-protocol.md`.

### Stop Decision, Counters and Limits

`runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` computes the whole stop decision once per pass from the config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. `deep-review.yaml` renders no stop arithmetic of its own. The frozen stop reasons are `converged`, `maxIterationsReached`, `blockedStop`, `manualStop`, `error`, `stuckRecovery`, `userPaused` and `maxDurationReached`. A missing graph decision blocks every non-terminal STOP.

The stuck count and the consecutive failure count are rebuilt from the state log on every pass, so a resumed run counts the same as one that never paused. Failed iterations stay out of the ratio signals. Three trailing failed iterations stop the loop with stop reason `error`. In confirm mode only, the workflow first asks the operator Retry, Skip or Cancel. Auto mode has no such question.

Each pass also compares `maxDurationMinutes` with the config `createdAt`. When the limit is reached the loop skips the dispatch and goes to synthesis with stop reason `maxDurationReached`.

### Expansive Depth Mode

- `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. An absent flag means `default`, which runs exactly as before.
- The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6 (the review root seed takes up to 8 depth-1 sub-targets, against 6 for research), a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to half of `maxIterations` unless the config sets a number. `antiConvergence.depthMode` is locked against the optimizer; the `expansiveDepth` caps are in neither its tunable nor its locked list.
- `step_frontier_seed` writes the depth-1 nodes before iteration 1 through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed --target review`, after `step_create_config` or before `step_fanout_spawn` on a fan-out run. A merge with no diff of its own is pruned `merge-no-own-diff` and an index rebuild `generated-artifact`.
- `step_frontier_admit` runs before the reducer and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, written only by `frontier-admit.cjs`; review has no inbox.
- `step_read_state` reads the active node and sets `next_dimension` to its next uncovered dimension, `step_frontier_activate` marks it active before dispatch, and the prompt pack's `{frontier_block}` names the node. Next Focus takes the blocked stop, then the pivot, then the node, then the leaf focus, then the first uncovered dimension.
- While a node stays open the frontier veto turns a stop candidate into `CONTINUE` with reasonDetail `frontierOpen`. It never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the review floor, and stuck recovery closes the active node `exhausted`.
- Once the frontier holds at least one answered node and none open or active, an expansive run that forms neither the clean nor the composite candidate gets the hard candidate `frontier_drained`, so a run whose frontier work is done stops instead of running to its cap; a fan-out lineage reads only its own partition. A frontier that closed with nothing answered forms no candidate. Every legal-stop gate and the graph decision still decide it, and a default run never forms it.
- A fan-out lineage keeps its coverage graph in `graph-db/` inside its own directory: the runner exports `DEEP_LOOP_COVERAGE_DB_DIR` to it, so the graph steps stay inside the lineage write surface and the hotspot saturation gate has a graph to read.
- A fan-out run deals the depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order, binds `frontier_scope:` beside `review_focus:`, records the partition as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json` and returns a partition guard for any node no lineage reports. Size each lineage's iteration cap (the `--iters=N` value on its executor entry, which `fanout-run.cjs` passes as that lineage's `--max-iterations`, 12 when unset) so half of it covers `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md:127:`--depth-mode=expansive` lets a run grow its own questions. A seed step writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1, and the leaf may propose children only when its prompt carries a frontier block. An admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, and prunes the proposal on the first failure. Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first and writes it as a frontier line, so visiting a node does not read as answering it. While a node stays open the frontier vetoes a soft convergence stop, which never outranks the iteration cap or the other hard stops, and the caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.

step_frontier_seed:
    description: "Seed the depth-1 frontier before any fan-out lineage spawns"
    enabled_when: "depth_mode == 'expansive'"
    skip_when: "config.fanout is absent"
    dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
    instructions: |
      Fan-out jumps from step_fanout_spawn to phase_synthesis and never reaches
      phase_init, so the seed runs here or not at all. fanout-run.cjs then splits
      the seeded depth-1 nodes round-robin across the lineages.
      Decompose {research_topic} into 3 to 6 depth-1 sub-questions that together
      cover the topic. Write them as a JSON array to {artifact_dir}/frontier-seed.json,
      one object per sub-question: {"question": "...", "scope": "<path, module or
      area>", "evidence": "<repo path or topic phrase that grounds it>"}. Then run
      the command. frontier-admit.cjs is the only writer of the node store and the
      frontier inbox rows; never write either file by hand.

    outputs:

    on_failure: "Halt with the command's stderr line. A run that asked for expansive depth must not start without its seeded frontier."

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '30,60p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md
| **Works on** | Web sources and repo files, read through the LEAF agent's tools |
| **Produces** | `research/research.md`, an iteration trail, a findings registry and a convergence dashboard |

### What It Does

`deep-research` runs one question through repeated rounds until the evidence stops growing. Every round starts a fresh `@deep-research` agent that reads the packet from disk, investigates one focus and writes its findings back. The workflow then reduces the state, scores novelty against the accumulated record and decides whether to continue. Start an unattended run with `/deep:research:auto "topic"`. Use `/deep:research:confirm "topic"` when you want an approval point before the first round and before synthesis.

It does not run a single-pass codebase lookup (`@context` does that) or audit code (`deep-review` does that).

### Why This Skill Exists

A long investigation inside one conversation loses detail as findings fill the context window. Pruning to keep the window small removes the early evidence. Reading prior results again costs the next round its working room. This skill writes every finding to disk, starts each round clean and stops on a measure of new information instead of a hunch.

---

## 2. QUICK START

**Step 1: Invoke it.** Auto resolves setup from the arguments and runs without approval prompts. Confirm asks for approval at setup and after each round. It also holds synthesis behind a gate.

```bash
/deep:research:auto "WebSocket reconnection strategies across browsers"
/deep:research:confirm "Distributed cache invalidation patterns"
```

Expected output: auto resolves setup without a prompt, writes the first packet files under `{spec_folder}/research/` and starts the loop. It asks one targeted question only when the spec folder is ambiguous. Confirm shows the consolidated setup prompt first and waits for your answers.

**Step 2: Run the primary workflow.** Bound the run with flags.

```bash
/deep:research:auto "API backpressure patterns" --max-iterations 6 --convergence 0.03
```

title: "Custom review dimensions"
description: "A review run accepts configured custom dimensions after the four canonical ones, tracks them in dimension coverage, and lets a covered custom dimension reach the all-dimensions-clean stop."
trigger_phrases:

# Custom review dimensions

## 1. OVERVIEW

A review normally audits four canonical dimensions: correctness, security, traceability and maintainability. A setup can name more. A configured name outside the canonical four is a custom dimension. It is tracked and covered like the others, ordered after the canonical four, and judged by its own name. It is not required for severity coverage.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Setup And Ordering

`.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml` accepts custom names at setup, normalizes them, and orders them after the canonical four in the risk order. The review registry seeds the four canonical keys plus the custom keys.

### Coverage Tracking

`runtime/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` derives each finding's dimension by matching it against the configured dimension universe, so a custom name that appears in the finding's focus is kept as its own dimension. When nothing matches, the derivation falls back to the iteration's first addressed dimension and then to `correctness`. `buildDimensionCoverage` builds coverage from the iteration records and the strategy dimensions.

### Review Contract And Agent

`deep-review/assets/review-mode-contract.yaml` carries a `customDimensionRule`: priority after the canonical four, configured order, judged by its own name under the shared P0/P1/P2 severity doctrine, and `requiredForSeverityCoverage: false`. The review agent definition allows a configured custom dimension under the same rubric rule.

### Stop Behavior

A covered custom dimension counts toward the all-dimensions-clean stop, so a review with every configured dimension covered and clean can reach that stop.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml` | Command workflow | Accepts, normalizes and orders custom names, and seeds the registry keys. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/reduce-state.cjs` | Script | Derives each finding's dimension against the configured universe and builds dimension coverage. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/assets/review-mode-contract.yaml` | Contract | The `customDimensionRule` block. |
| `.skilled/agents/deep-review.md` | Agent contract | Allows a configured custom dimension under the custom rubric rule. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/integration/review-depth-convergence.vitest.ts` | Vitest | The custom dimension stop-path case. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/tests/unit/deep-review-state-reducer.vitest.ts` | Vitest | Dimension coverage with custom names. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/manual-testing-playbook/initialization-and-state-setup/custom-dimension-setup-and-universe.md` | Manual playbook | DRV-074: a configured custom dimension joins the review dimension set. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/manual-testing-playbook/review-depth-v2-rollout/stop-gate-custom-dimension-coverage.md` | Manual playbook | DRV-075: a covered custom dimension reaches the all-dimensions-clean stop. |

Two live research pilots ran on one topic, and both failed the criterion that a stop candidate forms before the iteration cap: each run stopped on `maxIterationsReached`.

function buildStopDecisionInput(args, loopType, files, graph) {
  const config = isPlainObject(files?.config) ? files.config : {};
  const registry = isPlainObject(files?.registry) ? files.registry : {};
  const stateRows = Array.isArray(files?.stateLogRows) ? files.stateLogRows : [];
  const iterationRecords = orderedIterationRecords(stateRows);
  const evidenceRecords = iterationRecords.filter(
    (record) => record.status !== 'thought' && record.status !== 'error' && record.status !== 'timeout',
  );
  const ratioKey = loopType === 'review' ? 'newFindingsRatio' : 'newInfoRatio';
  const ratios = evidenceRecords
    .map((record) => record[ratioKey])
    .filter((value) => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value));
  const decisionConfig = stopDecisionConfig(config, loopType, args);
  const convergenceThreshold = decisionConfig.convergenceThreshold === null
    ? undefined
    : decisionConfig.convergenceThreshold;
  const counters = deriveLoopCounters(stateRows, { loopType, convergenceThreshold });

  return {
    loopType,
    iterationCount: stopDecisionNumberFlag(args.iterationCount, 'iterationCount')
      ?? iterationRecords.length,
    stuckCount: counters.stuckCount,
    consecutiveFailureCount: counters.consecutiveFailureCount,
    config: decisionConfig,
    ratios,
    research: loopType === 'research'
      ? stopDecisionResearchFacts(registry, evidenceRecords, graph)
      : null,
    review: loopType === 'review'
      ? stopDecisionReviewFacts({
        config,
        registry,

function frontierScopeRefs(scope) {
  if (Array.isArray(scope)) return asStringList(scope);
  const single = normalizeText(scope);
  return single ? [single] : [];

function mergeFrontierIntoSearchLedger(searchLedgerState, frontier) {
  const candidateCoverage = searchLedgerState.candidateCoverage;
  const debtById = new Map(
    (Array.isArray(searchLedgerState.searchDebt) ? searchLedgerState.searchDebt : [])
      .map((entry) => [entry.id, entry]),
  );
  for (const node of frontier.nodes) {
    const bugClass = `frontier:${node.nodeId}`;
    const iteration = node.iteration ?? 0;
    if (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active') {
      const id = `frontier-${node.nodeId}`;
      debtById.set(id, {
        id,
        iteration,
        targetRefs: frontierScopeRefs(node.scope),
        bugClass,
        disposition: 'deferred',
        rationale: `Frontier node ${node.nodeId} is still open: ${node.question}`,
        evidenceRefs: [`frontier-nodes.jsonl#${node.nodeId}`],
        reason: 'frontier node open',
        frontier: true,
      });
      addCandidateClass(candidateCoverage, bugClass, 'deferred', iteration);
    } else if (node.status === 'answered') {
      addCandidateClass(candidateCoverage, bugClass, 'covered', iteration);
    } else if (node.status === 'pruned' || node.status === 'abstained') {
      addCandidateClass(candidateCoverage, bugClass, 'ruled_out', iteration);
    }
  }
  searchLedgerState.searchDebt = [...debtById.values()]
    .sort((left, right) => left.iteration - right.iteration || left.id.localeCompare(right.id));
  for (const key of ['covered', 'ruledOut', 'deferred', 'blocked']) {
    candidateCoverage[key].sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));
  }
  for (const state of Object.values(candidateCoverage.byBugClass)) {
    state.iterations.sort((left, right) => Number(left) - Number(right));
  }
  return searchLedgerState;
}

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '14,35p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/completed-run-extension.md
## 1. OVERVIEW

`--extend=N` reopens a completed research run in the same session and generation and adds `N` iterations. The run keeps its `sessionId` and generation, records one `run_extended` ledger event, and keeps the prior synthesis as `research/synthesis-v{generation}-r{continuedFromRun}.md`. `--extend-minutes=M` adds minutes to the duration budget of the same extension.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Lineage And Preconditions

The extension uses lineage mode `completed-continue`. The run must already be complete. An unfinished run continues with `--lineage-mode=resume` instead. The extension is refused with fan-out.

### Iteration And Duration Budget

The iteration cap rises by `N` over the prior count. `--extend-minutes=M` is valid only with `--extend`. The new duration budget is `max(prior, elapsed) + M`, because the clock counts time since `createdAt`, so an extension started after the old budget still runs.

### Stop Interaction

Under `stopPolicy: convergence`, an extension with nothing new can stop again at once on `all_questions_answered`. Pass `--convergence-mode=off`, use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`, or add questions to `research/inbox.jsonl`.

### Ledger And Reducer Routing

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '1806,1860p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs
function buildNativeCommandInput(loopType, specFolder, lineageDir, lineage, options = {}) {
  assertActiveFanoutLoopType(loopType);
  const maxIterations = lineage.iterations || 12;
  const convergenceThreshold = options.convergenceThreshold ?? (loopType === 'research' ? 0.05 : 0.1);
  const stopPolicy = options.stopPolicy || 'convergence';
  const sessionId = options.sessionId || '';
  const reviewTarget = loopType === 'review' && options.reviewTarget ? options.reviewTarget : specFolder;
  const reviewTargetType = loopType === 'review' && options.reviewTargetType
    ? options.reviewTargetType
    : 'spec-folder';
  const args = [
    ':auto',
    JSON.stringify(loopType === 'review' ? reviewTarget : specFolder),
    `--spec-folder=${specFolder}`,
    `--max-iterations=${maxIterations}`,
    `--convergence=${convergenceThreshold}`,
    `--stop-policy=${stopPolicy}`,
    ...(options.convergenceMode ? [`--convergence-mode=${options.convergenceMode}`] : []),
    ...(options.depthMode === 'expansive' ? ['--depth-mode=expansive'] : []),
    `--fanout-lineage-artifact-dir=${lineageDir}`,
    '--lineage-mode=auto',
  ];
  if (loopType === 'research' && options.researchTopic) {
    args.push(`--research-topic=${JSON.stringify(options.researchTopic)}`);
  }
  const reviewFocus = loopType === 'review' && options.reviewFocus ? options.reviewFocus : null;
  if (reviewFocus) {
    args.push(`--review-focus=${JSON.stringify(reviewFocus)}`);
  }

  return [
    args.join(' '),
    '',
    'PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS:',
    `review_target: ${reviewTarget}`,
    `review_target_type: ${reviewTargetType}`,
    'review_dimensions: all',
    ...(reviewFocus ? [`review_focus: ${reviewFocus}`] : []),
    `spec_folder: ${specFolder}`,
    ...(sessionId ? [`session_id: ${sessionId}`] : []),
    'execution_mode: AUTONOMOUS',
    'lineage_mode: auto',
    `maxIterations: ${maxIterations}`,
    `convergenceThreshold: ${convergenceThreshold}`,
    `stop_policy: ${stopPolicy}`,
    ...(options.convergenceMode ? [`convergence_mode: ${options.convergenceMode}`] : []),
    ...(options.depthMode === 'expansive' ? ['depth_mode: expansive', `frontier_scope: ${(options.frontierScope || []).join(', ') || 'none'}`] : []),
    `config.fanout_lineage_artifact_dir: ${lineageDir}`,
  ].join('\n');
}

// ceiling and a positive override may only narrow it, never widen past it.
const LINEAGE_LIFETIME_HARD_MAX_HOURS = 4;

results.filter(isRecord).forEach((result, index) => {
    const status = normalizedKey(result.status);
    if (status === 'pass' || status === 'notapplicable' || status === 'not_applicable' || status === 'not applicable' || status === 'n/a') return;
    const protocol = normalizeText(result.protocol || result.id) || `protocol ${index + 1}`;
    const refs = readStringList(result.evidenceRefs);
    add({
      source: 'traceability-gap',
      title: `Re-examine traceability protocol: ${protocol}`,
      focus: `Read-only re-examine the configured ${protocol} traceability protocol and report unresolved drift without changing the review target.`,
      evidenceRefs: refs.length ? refs : [`deep-review-findings-registry.json#traceability-result-${index + 1}`],
      relevanceRationale: 'The reducer-owned traceability rollup does not record this configured protocol as passing or not applicable.',
      scopeGrounded: true,
    });
  });

  // A frontier node the run closed without answering is review depth it already
  // paid for, and revisiting that sub-target is read-only work inside the same
  // configured target, so it seeds a fresh direction instead of being dropped.
  expansiveFrontierNodes(config, registry).forEach((node) => {
    const status = normalizeText(node.status);
    if (status !== 'exhausted' && status !== 'blocked') return;
    const nodeId = normalizeText(node.nodeId);
    const scope = (Array.isArray(node.scope)
      ? readStringList(node.scope).join(', ')
      : normalizeText(node.scope)) || 'unscoped';
    add({
      source: 'frontier-unanswered',
      title: `Revisit ${status} frontier node ${nodeId}`,
      focus: `Read-only review of the ${status} frontier sub-target ${nodeId} (${scope}) and report findings without expanding the review target.`,
      evidenceRefs: [`frontier-nodes.jsonl#${nodeId}`],
      relevanceRationale: `The frontier closed this sub-target ${status} without covering every configured dimension.`,
      scopeGrounded: true,
    });
  });

  return seeds;
}

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '14,40p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/anti-convergence-floor.md
## 1. OVERVIEW

Prevents convergence STOP before the configured minimum iteration floor while preserving hard stops and pause behavior.

The anti-convergence floor keeps deep-research from ending after a shallow early signal. Operators get a declarative `minIterations` default and a `convergenceMode` escape hatch without weakening max-iteration, pause, or halt boundaries.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

The config template defines `antiConvergence.minIterations` with a default of `3` and `antiConvergence.convergenceMode` with a default of `default`. The workflow writes those values into the config record, validates older or malformed configs at read time, and clamps the effective floor to the hard `maxIterations` cap when needed.

During convergence checking, a STOP candidate is blocked while the completed iteration count is below the effective floor. When the floor clears, the workflow emits `min_iterations_guard_pass`, keeping attribution separate from quality-guard events. `convergenceMode: "off"` disables convergence-driven STOP while leaving max iterations, pause, and other explicit halts active.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/assets/deep-research-config.json` | Asset | Defines the anti-convergence defaults and locked config fields. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` | Workflow | Writes the floor into the config and emits floor events; convergence.cjs enforces the floor before a STOP. |

### Validation And Tests
[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '18,45p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/divergent-convergence-mode.md
---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

The workflow admits the exact reasons `composite_converged` and `all_questions_answered`. A successful pivot selects a new focus from persisted adjacent questions, contradiction or verification gaps, missing source classes, alternate evidence methods, and independent finding checks. The shared adapter runs three native seats in one round. A first-attempt quorum miss gets a one-time quorum retry of the same three seats, so one pivot uses at most six seat outputs. The adapter persists the transaction under `<artifactRoot>/divergent/pivots/<pivotId>/council/**`, restores the selected focus, and continues the loop.

`maxIterationsReached`, `blockedStop`, `stuckRecovery`, `minIterationsNotReached`, `error`, `manualStop`, `userPaused` and `maxDurationReached` do not pivot. A pivot failure follows the existing synthesis path.

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` | Workflow | Routes an eligible stop returned by convergence.cjs into native seat dispatch, continuation, and fail-closed synthesis. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/divergent-research-pivot.ts` | Mode adapter | Builds bounded research candidates and the three research seat mandates. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/lib/deep-loop/divergent-pivot.ts` | Shared runtime | Owns the mechanics-only pivot transaction, event vocabulary, quorum, agreement, and artifact layout. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` | Shared runtime | Validates the four-value convergence-mode enum without translating STOP decisions. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/convergence/convergence.md` | Reference | Defines the operator-facing convergence-mode and cost contract. |

[0m$ [0mcd /Users/michelkerkmeester/worktrees/public/455-expansive-depth-integration && sed -n '20,30p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/feature-catalog/severity-system/cross-mode-anti-convergence-contract.md
---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

The review config declares the floor in `assets/deep-review-config.json`. Its optimizer-managed block marks `antiConvergence.convergenceMode`, `antiConvergence.depthMode` and `antiConvergence.stopPolicy` as locked fields while keeping the review loop's ordinary tunables (`convergenceThreshold`, `stuckThreshold`, `maxIterations`) separate.

The runtime capability matrix declares `stopPolicy: "fail-closed"`. The shared runtime capability resolver rejects any matrix where `stopPolicy` is absent or not `fail-closed`, so callers cannot silently fall back to permissive stop behavior.

The optimizer manifest carries an invariant group for the same contract: `antiConvergence.convergenceMode` and `convergenceMode` stay locked, and `antiConvergence.minIterations` is tunable only when the paired `minIterations <= maxIterations` constraint holds.

The evidence that prompt-hardening alone drove DeepSeek to zero breaches is why the heavier `prevent-not-revert` write-jail was ruled out. Executor rosters, allowlists, dispatch env construction, convergence and stop-policy logic are unchanged. Every mode's ledger/reducer/certificate surface is unchanged too. No behavior changes for strong-model executors.

function loadPivotEventRecords(researchDir) {
  const pivotsRoot = path.join(researchDir, 'divergent', 'pivots');
  if (!fs.existsSync(pivotsRoot)) {
    return [];
  }

  return fs.readdirSync(pivotsRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory())
    .sort((left, right) => {
      const leftOrdinal = Number(left.name.match(/^pivot-(\d+)-/)?.[1] ?? 0);
      const rightOrdinal = Number(right.name.match(/^pivot-(\d+)-/)?.[1] ?? 0);
      return leftOrdinal - rightOrdinal || left.name.localeCompare(right.name);
    })
    .flatMap((entry) => {
      const statePath = path.join(pivotsRoot, entry.name, 'council', 'state.jsonl');
      if (!fs.existsSync(statePath)) {
        return [];
      }
      return readUtf8(statePath)
        .split(/\r?\n/)
        .map((line) => line.trim())
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((line, index) => {
          let record;
          try {
            record = JSON.parse(line);
          } catch (error) {
            const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
            throw new Error(`Invalid pivot state at ${statePath}:${index + 1}: ${message}`);
          }
          return {
            ...record,
            pivotArtifactRef: path.relative(researchDir, statePath),
          };
        });
    });
}

function buildPivotState(eventRecords) {
  const started = eventRecords.filter((record) => record.event === 'pivot_started');
  const selected = eventRecords.filter((record) => record.event === 'pivot_selected');
  const completed = eventRecords.filter((record) => record.event === 'pivot_completed');
  const failed = eventRecords.filter((record) => record.event === 'pivot_failed');
  const overrides = eventRecords.filter((record) => record.event === 'pivot_override_accepted');
  const sourceIterationByPivot = new Map(started.map((record) => [
    record.pivotId,
    isFiniteNumber(record.source?.sourceIteration) ? record.source.sourceIteration : null,
  ]));
  const selectedCandidateByPivot = new Map(selected.map((record) => [record.pivotId, record.selectedCandidate]));

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Derive the deterministic pivot id from immutable lineage and normalized trigger data. */
export function derivePivotId(input: PivotIdentityInput): string {
  const identity = validateIdentity(input);
  const digestInput = [
    identity.sessionId,
    identity.generation,
    identity.loopType,
    identity.sourceIteration,
    identity.normalizedTrigger,
  ].join('|');
  const digest = createHash('sha256').update(digestInput).digest('hex').slice(0, 12);
  return `pivot-${identity.ordinal}-${digest}`;
}
